# Ae Layer Properties | Automation Blocks

[Skip to main content](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#)

On this page

Ae Layer Properties
===================

### Layer Properties vs Layer Attributes[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_layer_properties_vs_attributes "Direct link to heading")

Layer properties are the parameters of a layer which are listed in the After Effects timeline panel and that can usually be keyframed. There are many other things that belong to a layer, like its name, motion blur setting, parent layer or in point, for example - and we call those _attributes_. This chapter is only about layer properties - the blocks to [get](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer#ae_layer_get_attribute)
 or [set a layer attribute](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer#ae_layer_set_attribute)
 are described in the section [Layers](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer)
.

![Properties vs Attributes of Layers](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockOther/layer_properties_vs_attributes.png)

### Accessing Layer Properties[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_layer_properties_access "Direct link to heading")

Whenever a block requires a layer property as argument, Automation Blocks represents the property as a [text](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text)
, which is similar to a file path. You can create those texts manually using a [Text block](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text#text)
, for example, but usually it is more convenient to use a [Layer Property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_path)
 block, which will create the text for you.

A property path string is identical to a [layer path string](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer#accessLayer)
, except that you need to append the match name or name of the property and all its parent properties (i.e. groups that it is included in).

The position is inside the Transform group, for example, so the path for the position of the layer _"White Solid 1"_ in _"Comp 1"_ is

`Comp 1::White Solid 1/Transform/Position`

Note that this will only work in the English version of Ae, and in other language versions you have to use the translated terms for "Transform" and "Position". To be language independent, each property in Ae also has a [match name](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_match_name)
. The match name for _"Position"_ is _"ADBE Position"_ and the one for _"Transform"_ is _"ADBE Transform Group"_. So you can also access the position as:

`/Comp 1::White Solid 1/ADBE Transform Group/ADBE Position`

However, the good news is that you rarely need to create these paths yourself. Usually, you use the Layer Property block instead.

Layer Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_path "Direct link to heading")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_path](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_path.png)

This block will create the path strings for you, which you need to access a particular property. If you select the position property of the layer "White Solid 1" of the comp "Comp 1", for example, and then click the _refresh_ icon of the block, it will create this path for you:

`/Comp 1::White Solid 1/ADBE Transform Group/ADBE Position`

which is exactly the path you need to access this property.

Property Value[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_value_json "Direct link to heading")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_value_json](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_value_json.png)

If you want to [set a property value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_value)
 or a [keyframe](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeKeyframes#ae_set_value_at_time)
, this value can be as simple as a single number (for the _Opacticy_ property), or as complex as a shape or mask path. With this block you can store all kinds of property values as part of your script. Just select a property and click the _refresh_ button of the block. It will read the property value and store it in the block as a string in JSON format. Now you can use it to set the value of a property using the block [Set Attribute of Property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute)
 for example.

Note that the block creates a string in JSON format. If you feel adventurous, you can copy and paste this data to a code editor of your choice and understand its structure. Then you can also create and manipulate these json texts with the blocks in the [text](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text)
 category.

Only Selected Layer Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_only_selected_property "Direct link to heading")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_only_selected_property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_only_selected_property.png)

Returns the (full path of) the only currently selected property of the given composition. If more than one property is selected, the block will stop with an error.

List of Layer Properties[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_list "Direct link to heading")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_list.png)

This block creates a [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
 (array) of all layer properties of the given layer(s) or composition(s). You can give this block either a single layer or comp or an entire list of layers or comps as argument and it will put the properties of all of them into the list.

You can choose to only include certain kinds of properties into the list. For a description of the available filter options, see the block [For Each Layer Property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_for_all)
, which has exactly the same options.

For Each Layer Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_for_all "Direct link to heading")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_for_all](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_for_all.png)

This block is a [loop](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/loops)
 which allows you to do something with all (or some of) the properties of the given layer(s) or comp(s). The blocks you plug into this block are executed many times - namely once for each of the considered properties. In the first row, you'll find various filter options to choose which properties should be considered.

**my property variable**

The block creates a [variable](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/variables)
 `my property` and during each execution of the loop, the variable contains the property that is currently considered.

**comp(s), layer(s) or group(s)**

At this parameter, which is set to the [active comp](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeComp#ae_active_comp)
 by default, you can either specify a [single comp](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeComp#accessComp)
 or [layer](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer#accessLayer)
 or [Layer Property Group](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_path)
 or a [list of layers](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer#ae_layer_list)
 or [comps](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeProjectItems#block_ae_get_items_in_bin)
. For each given layer, all properties of that layer are considered - for each given composition, the properties of all layers in that comp are considered. If you give a property group (like the _Transform_ group of a layer, for example) only the properties contained in that group are considered.

**each**

In the first dropdown list of the block you have the option to loop over `each` or `each selected` property.

**property type filter**

In the second dropdown list of the block you can limit the loop to different kinds of properties:

*   `property and property group` loops over all properties, including the groups which contain other properties (example: the loop is not just executed for properties like the _Position_ or _Scale_ property inside the _Transform_ group, but also for the _Transform_ group itself).
*   `property` loops over all properties except groups (example: the loop is executed for _Position_ and _Scale_ inside the _Transform_ group, but not for the _Transform_ group itself)
*   **Type Filter:** For each [property value type](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_property_value_type)
     there is a separate entry in the dropdown which allows you to loop only over properties with that type (like for each `color property` or `2D spatial property`, for example).
*   `keyframed property` loops only over properties which currently have keyframes.
*   `keyframeable property` loops only over properties which can be keyframed (whether they currently [have keyframes](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_num_keys)
     or not).
*   `property with expression` loops only over properties which have a (non-empty) expression
*   `property to which expressions can be applied` loops only over properties to which expressions can be applied (whether they currently have an expression or not)

For Each Effect[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#for_each_effect "Direct link to heading")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block for_each_effect](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/for_each_effect.png)

Loops over all the effects of the given comp(s) or layer(s). You can either give the block a single comp or a single layer as argument or an entire [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
 (array) of them. The property group of the effect is stored in the variable `my effect` when the nested blocks of the loop are executed.

Get Attribute of Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_get_attribute "Direct link to heading")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_get_attribute](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_get_attribute.png)

Returns one of the attributes of the given layer property - most notably the property's [value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_value)
, but also other attributes like its expression, its name or number of keyframes. For a description of all available attributes see [Set Attribute of Property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute)
.

Set Attribute of Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute "Direct link to heading")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_set_attribute](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_set_attribute.png)

Sets any of the available attributes of a property - like its [value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_value)
, expression, name or selected flag. After you choose an attribute, the inputs of the block change as needed for that attribute.

The available attributes of properties are listed below. If an attribute is marked as read-only, you can only get but not set this attribute.

### can set enabled (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_can_set_enabled "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is `true`, if you can set the [enabled](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_enabled)
 attribute value. Generally, this is true if the user interface displays an eyeball icon for this property;

### can set expression (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_can_set_expression "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is `true`, if this property supports expressions.

### can vary over time (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_can_vary_over_time "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is `true`, if that property can vary over time—that is, keyframe values or expressions can be written to this property.

If you want to know if the property is actually varying over time, use the attribute [is time varying](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_is_time_varying)
.

### dimensions separated[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#dimensions-separated "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 `true` or `false`. When true, the property's dimensions are represented as separate properties. For example, if the layer's position is represented as X Position and Y Position properties in the Timeline panel, the Position property has this attribute set to true.

### dropdown list entries (write only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#dropdown-list-entries-write-only "Direct link to heading")

A [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
 (array) of texts. This attribute can only be set for one property, namely the **Dropdown Menu Control** effect. It allows to set the labels for the dropdown options, in the same way as if you would click the _Edit_ button of this effect.

![Setting dropdown list entries of the Dropdown Menu Control](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockOther/ae_property_set_attribute_dropdown_list_entries.png)

To insert an separator in the list, add an empty string to the like (or alternatively the text `(-`). The `|` symbol is not allowed in label names and will be removed. If you try to set the dropdown list entries for other properties than the _Dropdown Menu Control_ effect, a warning is printed on the console and the request is ignored.

##### Matchname Change

If you change the dropdown list entries, After Effects internally replaces the effect by a new one with a new match name. As a result, any variable pointing to the effect becomes invalid. If you need to do multiple things with the effect (say setting its list entries AND changing its name, adding keyframes,...) best change the list entries after the other operations. If this is not possible, you can also save the name of the

#### example[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#example "Direct link to heading")

In [this example](https://www.dropbox.com/s/fj2wxz56b1tgk9u/Create%20Dropdown%20Effect%2C%20Change%20Choices%20and%20Choose%20One.xml?dl=0)
 we first create a dropdown list on the only selected layer. Then we set its dropdown list options, and after that change the name of the dropdown and choose the fourth of the new list options:

![Example: create dropdown effect, change its labels and pick one of them.](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockOther/ae_property_set_attribute_dropdown_list_entries_example.png)

### enabled[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_enabled "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 `true` or `false`. For a layer, this corresponds to the video switch state of the layer in the Timeline panel. For an effect and other properties, it corresponds to the setting of the eyeball icon, if there is one.

If you try to set this attribute for a property whose [can set enabled](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_can_set_enabled)
 attribute is `false`, Automation Blocks writes a warning to the console and ignores the request (but continues the script execution and does _not_ stop with an error).

### expression[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression "Direct link to heading")

The expression (as a [text](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text)
) which is applied to this property (or the empty text, if there is no expression).

If you set this value, the following happens:

*   If the text contains a valid expression, [expression enabled](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_enabled)
     becomes `true`.
*   If the text does not contain a valid expression, an [error](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_error)
     is generated, and [expression enabled](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_enabled)
     becomes `false`.
*   If you set the attribute to the empty text, [expression enabled](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_enabled)
     becomes `false` but no error is generated.

If you try to set this attribute for a property whose [can set expression](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_can_set_expression)
 attribute is `false`, Automation Blocks writes a warning to the console and ignores the request (but continues the script execution and does _not_ stop with an error).

### expression enabled[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_enabled "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 `true` or `false` which indicates whether the expression of this property is currently active or not.

### expression error (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_error "Direct link to heading")

Contains the error message [text](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text)
, if any, generated by evaluation of the [expression](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression)
 on this property. If the property has no expression, or if the expression evaluated without error, this contains the empty text.

### has max value (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_has_max_value "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is `true` if the property has a maximum value (i.e. its [max value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_max_value)
 attribue is not [null](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic#logic_null)
.

Note that when you attempt to set a value, Automation Blocks clips it to the min-max range of the property, if it has one. So, you usually don't need to worry about these.

### has index (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_has_index "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 `true` or `false` which determined whether this property is part of an "indexed group", i.e. if it can be moved up or down among its sibling properties. Effects have an index, for example (you can move an effect up or down in the effects stack), but the "Transform" group or the "Effects" groups, which contains all the effects, has a fixed place and cannot be moved, so they have no index.

If a property has an index, you can get or set it using the [index](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_index)
 attribute.

### has min value (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_has_min_value "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is analogous to the [has max value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_has_max_value)
 attribute.

### max value (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_max_value "Direct link to heading")

Some properties have a min-max range: The opacity of a layer cannot be smaller than 0 or bigger than 100. This attribute is [null](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic#logic_null)
 if this property has no maximum value. See also [has max value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_has_max_value)
.

**min/max for array values**

Also, some array values have minimal or maximal values. The size of a rectangle shape is a 2D value, for example, and has a minumum of 0. Note that the minimum is just a single number and applies to all components of the array, i.e. neither the width nor the height can be smaller than zero.

### min value (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_min_value "Direct link to heading")

This attribute is analogous to the [max value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_max_value)
 attribute.

### index[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_index "Direct link to heading")

The index of the property, which determines where the index is located among its siblings. The first property in a group has index 1.

You can set this value to a new number to move the property up and down. Setting the index of an effect to 1, for example, makes it the first effect on that layer, but you can also use it on masks or shape groups, for example.

_positive number_ If you set the value to `8`, it will become the 8th property of the group (unless the group has less than 8 layers: in that case it will become the last property). If you set the value to `0` the result is the same as if you set it to `1` - it will become the first property of the group.

_negative number_ If you set this value to a negative number, the index is counted from the end of the group. Hence, if you set the value to `-1`, it will become the last property of the group and if you set it to `-2`, it will become the last but one.

_not everything has an index_

You can move an effect up and down, but you cannot move individual controls of an effect up and down (since those have a fixed order inside the effect). To check if a property can be moved, see the attribute [has index](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_has_index)
. If you try to get the index of a property which has no index, the result will be [null](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic#logic_null)
.

### is time varying (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_is_time_varying "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 `true` or `false`. When `true`, the property is time varying — that is, it has keyframes or an [enabled](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression_enabled)
 [expression](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_expression)
. When this attribute is `true`, the attribute [can vary over time](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_can_vary_over_time)
 must also be `true`.

### name[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#name "Direct link to heading")

The name of the property. Note that not all names can be set. You can, for example, rename an effect that you applied to a layer, but you cannot rename the group `Effects` in which it is contained:

![After Effects scripting rename property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockOther/ae_property_set_attribute_name_rename.png)

If you attempt to rename a property which cannot be renamed, Automation Blocks ignores the request, prints a warning to the console and continues with the execution of the script (and does _not_ stop with an error).

### name (unique match name) (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_match_name "Direct link to heading")

A special name for the property is used to [build unique naming paths](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_layer_properties_access)
. The match name is not displayed, but you can refer to it in scripts. Every property has a unique match-name identifier. Match names are stable from version to version regardless of the display name (the name attribute value) or any changes to the application. Unlike the display name, it is not localized. An indexed group may not have a name value, but always has a matchName value.

### num keys (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_num_keys "Direct link to heading")

The number of keyframes of this property (0 if the property is not keyframed).

### num properties (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_num_properties "Direct link to heading")

If this property is a group, this is the number of properties that are contained within it. For the property `::1/ADBE Effect Parade` (which is the _Effects_ group of the first layer of the active comp), it returns the number of effects on that layer. If the property is no group, the value is 0.

### property value type (read-only)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_property_value_type "Direct link to heading")

A [text](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text)
 describing the kind of [value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_value)
, which this property has. This text can have the following values:

*   `NO_VALUE`: (groups, like the "Transform Group", for example)
*   `ThreeD_SPATIAL`: a [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
     (array) of three numbers representing a point in 3D space (the position of a layer, for example)
*   `ThreeD`: a [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
     (array) of three numbers (the scale of a layer, for example)
*   `TwoD_SPATIAL` a [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
     (array) of two numbers representing a point in 2D space(the _Point_ property of the _Point Control_ effect, for example)
*   `TwoD`: a [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
     (array) of two numbers (the _Size_ of _Rectangle Path_ of a _Shape Layer_, for example)
*   `OneD`: a single number, (like the _Opacity_ or _Rotation_ of a layer, for example)
*   `COLOR`: a [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
     (array) of 4 floating-point numbers. You can set color values in various formats, see [Ae Color](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeColor)
    .
*   `CUSTOM_VALUE` these values cannot be read or set by scripts (the _Histogram_ property of the _Levels_ effect, for example).
*   `MARKER`: an index number refering to a marker on the same layer as the property itself (starting with 1 for the first marker).
*   `LAYER_INDEX`: The index of a layer in the same comp. If you want to set this value, you can also [specify the layer in other ways](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayer#accessLayer)
    , and Automation Blocks will convert it to an index for you.
*   `MASK_INDEX`: The index of a mask on the same layer (1 for the first mask on that layer).
*   `SHAPE`: A shape path as a json text.
*   `TEXT_DOCUMENT`: A [text](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/text)
    .

**spatial vs non-spatial**

One difference between spatial and non-spatial values is that spatial values are usually visualized as points in the _Composition_ panel (you can click and drag a point control in the comp viewer, for example). But the most important difference is that the interpolation between keyframes is very different for spatial and non-spatial properties. Non-spatial properties only have temporal interpolation - they are interpolated exactly as if they where two (or three) independent 1D properties. By contrast, spatial properties have a spatial interpolation, which defines the path along which the value travels from keyframe to keyframe. Temporal interpolation thus is not performed for each dimension individually, but only once, along the path.

You can see the difference when you separate the dimensions of the position property, because this effectively switches from spatial interpolation to non-spatial interpolation:

**more 3D than you can see**

Note that After Effects sometimes hides part of a value from the user interface. Layer positions are always 3D spatial values, for example, even if the layer is a 2D layer. The third component is hidden (and ignored) in that case, but still present. The same is also the case for the scale property.

### selected[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#selected "Direct link to heading")

A [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is `true`, if the property is selected.

### value[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_value "Direct link to heading")

The value of the property. If the property has keyframes, the value at the current time is returned if you get the value and a keyframe is created at the current time if you set the value.

See the [Property Value Type](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_set_attribute_property_value_type)
 to figure out what kind of value a property has. If you want to set the value of a property, you can create this value with the [Property Value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_value_json)
 block.

If you want to keyframe a value, see the block [Add Keyframes](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeKeyframes#ae_keyframes_apply)

Delete Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_remove "Direct link to heading")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_remove](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_remove.png)

Deletes the given property (or [list](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/lists)
 (array) of properties). If the property is a group, all child properties are removed as well. Note that not all properties can be deleted - you can delete an effect, for example, but you cannot delete an individual parameter of an effect or the transform group.

Rebase Property[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_rebase "Direct link to heading")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_rebase](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_rebase.png)

This block allows you to replace the comp or layer of a layer property string. In other words, it returns the same property, but on a different layer or on the same layer in a different comp.

If you access a property with the [Layer Property](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_path)
 block, for example, it contains the full path for the property, like

`comp 1::my text layer/transform/position`

for the position property of the layer _"my text layer"_ in the composition _"comp 1"_. If you want to access the position of another layer, you can take this path and replace the layer, which will effectively replace the `comp 1::my text layer` part of the path with the respective path from the new layer.

After you have used the _Rebase Property_ block, you'll often want to check if the [property exists](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_exists)
, because not all layers have the same properties.

Property Exists[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_exists "Direct link to heading")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block ae_property_exists](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/AeLayerProperties/ae_property_exists.png)

This block returns a [logic value](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/logic)
 which is `true` if the given property exists. You can always assume that a video layer has a position property, for example, but if you want to access a property of an effect, which may or may not be applied to the layer, you should check if the property actually exists before you try to [get an attribute](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_property_get_attribute)
 from it, for example.

See also the [Project Item Exists](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeProjectItems#ae_item_exists)
 block.

##### Check if layer exists

Since layers are also properties, you can also use the _Property Exists_ block to check if a particular layer exists.

*   [Layer Properties vs Layer Attributes](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/aeLayerProperties/#ae_layer_properties_vs_attributes)

# Remote Execution | Automation Blocks

[Skip to main content](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#)

On this page

Remote Execution
================

Automation Blocks tools can be launched in all kinds of ways, using keyboard shortcuts, external devices, your phone, or other software running on your machine. There are two methods to do that:

*   _HTTP Requests_ are great to lauch AB scripts with keyboard shortcuts (using [Auto Hotkey](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#autohotkey)
     on Windows or [Automator](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#automator)
     or [Keyboard Maestro](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#keyboardmaestro)
     on Mac OS). It is also useful to trigger Automation Blocks with external devices like [Stream Deck](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#streamdeck)
     or your phone using [Touch Portal](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#touchportal)
    .
*   _CEP Events_ can be used to launch AB scripts from other extensions in Ae and Pr. This also includes script launchers like [KBar and Quick Menu](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#kbarquickmemu)
    .

Remote Execution via HTTP Requests[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#remote-execution-via-http-requests "Direct link to heading")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

With Automation Blocks, you have the power to execute any automation remotely using HTTP requests. This feature enables seamless integration with external devices such as Elgato Stream Deck, Touch Portal, Keyboard Maestro on Mac, Auto Hotkey on Windows, and various other trigger-based software packages.

### Enabling HTTP Request Execution[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#enabling-http-request-execution "Direct link to heading")

To activate HTTP request execution for your Automation Blocks scripts, follow these steps:

1.  Navigate to Settings: Open the Automation Blocks extension and navigate to the settings panel.
    
2.  Toggle HTTP Request Execution: Within the settings panel, locate the option to toggle HTTP request execution on or off. Enable this option to allow remote execution of scripts via HTTP requests.
    
3.  Configure Port (Optional): Additionally, you have the option to configure the port used for HTTP requests. By default, Automation Blocks uses a predefined port (7741 for Pr and 7742 for Ae), but you can customize this to suit your preferences. Simply specify the desired port number in the preferences section.
    
4.  Restart Host Application: After making any changes to the HTTP request settings, it's important to restart the host application (Premiere Pro or After Effects) for the changes to take effect.
    

![Enable Remote Execution in Automation Blocks](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/remote_execution_settings.png)

### How It Works[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#howitworks "Direct link to heading")

Once enabled, you can trigger your Automation Blocks scripts by simply opening a specific URL. Here's how it works:

#### Executing the Current Automation Blocks Script:[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#executing-the-current-automation-blocks-script "Direct link to heading")

If you want to execute the Automation Blocks tool currently open in the Automation Blocks panel (assuming that you've chosen port 7741), simply open the following URL:

    http://localhost:7741/runCurrentAutomationBlocksScript

#### Executing a Script from a File:[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#executing-a-script-from-a-file "Direct link to heading")

If you have a script saved in a file, such as C:\\myTools\\sampleTool.xml, you can execute it by opening the URL:

    http://localhost:7741/runAutomationBlocksScript?filePath=C:\myTools\sampleTool.xml

This functionality even works if the Automation Blocks panel is not open, though the host application must be running.

#### Viewing Remote URLs:[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#viewing-remote-urls "Direct link to heading")

In the preferences, you'll find the option to "View Remote URLs". Opening the following URL in your web browser will display a web page listing all tools in your Automation Blocks library along with their respective URLs for execution:

    http://localhost:7741/listAutomationBlocksScripts

This feature provides convenient access to all available scripts and their corresponding URLs.

### Auto Hotkey (Win)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#autohotkey "Direct link to heading")

[AutoHotkey](https://www.autohotkey.com/)
 is a free and open-source scripting language for Windows that enables users to create macros, automate repetitive tasks, and remap keys and buttons, making it a versatile tool for automating workflows and customizing system behavior.

#### Configure Hotkey[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#configure-hotkey "Direct link to heading")

Here is a sample Auto Hotkey V2 script, which executes an Automation Blocks script file `C:\Users\info\OneDrive\Dokumente\Automation Blocks Pr Library\HelloWorld.xml` with the F11 key (adjust port according to port configured in your AB prefs):

    ; Define a hotkey for F11F11::{    ; enter here the URL of the Automation Blocks tool you want to trigger:    url := 'http://localhost:7741/runAutomationBlocksScript?filePath=C:\Users\info\OneDrive\Dokumente\Automation%20Blocks%20Pr%20Library\HelloWorld.xml'    whr := ComObject("WinHttp.WinHttpRequest.5.1")    whr.Open("GET", url, False), whr.Send()  }

Just update the line `url := ...` with the remote url of the Automation Blocks tool you want to trigger.

#### Limit Hotkey to be Active Only when Pr/Ae has Focus[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#limit-hotkey-to-be-active-only-when-prae-has-focus "Direct link to heading")

Note that the script above will run the Automation Blocks tool whenever the F11 key is pressed - also if Premiere Pro is just running in the background and anoth application has the focus. If you want the shortcut to only be active when Premiere Pro has focus, you can change the script like that:

    ; Define a hotkey for F11F11::{    if WinActive("Premiere Pro"){                ; enter here the URL of the Automation Blocks tool you want to trigger:        url := 'http://localhost:7741/runAutomationBlocksScript?filePath=C:\Users\info\OneDrive\Dokumente\Automation%20Blocks%20Pr%20Library\HelloWorld.xml'    whr := ComObject("WinHttp.WinHttpRequest.5.1")    whr.Open("GET", url, False), whr.Send()      }  }

The WinActive command ensures that the url trigger is only executed if a window with "Premiere Pro" in its title is in focus. Of course, you ned to replace "Premiere Pro" by "After Effecs" if you configure hotkeys for Ae.

Note that you can define multiple keyboard shortcuts in a single Auto Hotkey script:

    ; Define a hotkey for F11F11::{        ; code for F11 hotkey goes here}; Define a hotkey for Ctrl+Shift+A^+a::{        ; code for Ctrl+Shift+A hotkey goes here}

### Automator (Mac)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#automator "Direct link to heading")

Automator for Mac OS is a built-in application that allows users to automate repetitive tasks by creating custom workflows or shortcuts, streamlining processes and saving time. Since Automator is installed by default on any Mac, it allows you to execute Automation Blocks tools with keyboard shortcuts without the need to install any third party tools.

To setup a new shortcut for an Automation Blocks Remote URL, follow these steps.

1.  Open Automator and create a new Quick Action.

![](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/example_automator_quick_action_1.png)

2.  In the Quick Action choose the action "Run Shell Script" and enter as script the command `curl` followed by the URL you want to trigger:![](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/example_automator_quick_action_2.png)

The command to execute the currently open Automation Blocks script, for example, would be:

    curl http://localhost:7741/runCurrentAutomationBlocksScript

(For more URLs see the section [How it Works](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#howitworks)
)

Also make sure to set the option that the workflow receives no input and choose as target application Premiere Pro (or choose target "any application" if the keyboard shortcut should also work when any other applicataion has the focus). Save the quick action with a meaningful name - in our example we use "Automation Blocks Pr Run Current Script"

Once it is saved, you find the quick action in the main menu of Premiere Pro at "Premiere Pro->Services"; so you can check now if it executes the Automation Blocks script properly. In the remaining steps we configure that the quick action can be triggered with a keyboard shortcut.

3.  Open the Mac OS System Settings, open the tab "Keyboard", and choose "Keyboard Shortcuts..."

![](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/example_automator_quick_action_3.png)

4.  Open the tab "App Shortcuts" and click on the + at the bottom of the list to add a new shortcut. In the dialog which pops up, choose as _Application_ Adobe Premiere Pro and enter as Menu Title the exact name of your quick action (as it is listed in the main menu of Pr at "Premiere Pro->Services"). Then enter as keyboad shortcut the shortcut you want to use.

![](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/example_automator_quick_action_4.png)

### Keyboard Maestro (Mac)[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#keyboardmaestro "Direct link to heading")

[Keyboard Maestro](https://www.keyboardmaestro.com/main/)
 is a powerful automation tool for macOS that allows users to create macros and automate repetitive tasks using keyboard shortcuts, triggers, and actions, providing advanced automation capabilities for productivity and efficiency.

To execute an Automation Blocks script, just open the respective URL with the "_Get URL_" action of Keyboard Maestro:

![Execute Automation Blocks script with Keyboard Maestro in Premiere Pro and After Effects](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/example_keyboard_maestro.png)

#### Triggers[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#triggers "Direct link to heading")

Keyboard Maestro offers a [wide range of triggers](https://wiki.keyboardmaestro.com/Triggers)
, including [hotkey triggers](https://wiki.keyboardmaestro.com/trigger/Hot_Key)
 like the one demonstrated above, which executes the Automation Blocks tool when Ctrl+Shift+A is pressed. Alternatively, you can utilize a [Folder trigger](https://wiki.keyboardmaestro.com/trigger/Folder)
 to create watch folder functionality, or a [Mounted Volume trigger](https://wiki.keyboardmaestro.com/trigger/Mounted_Volume)
 to automatically execute an AB script when connecting your camera's memory card to your computer.

#### Limit Hotkey to be Active Only when Pr/Ae has Focus[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#limit-hotkey-to-be-active-only-when-prae-has-focus-1 "Direct link to heading")

Macros in Keyboard Maestro are organized in [macro groups](https://wiki.keyboardmaestro.com/Macro_Groups)
 and for each macro group you can configure that it is only active when a particular application has focus. That way, you can ensure that the keyboard shortcuts you configure are only active when Premiere Pro has focus, for example.

### Stream Deck[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#streamdeck "Direct link to heading")

Stream Deck is a customizable control surface by Elgato that allows users to create personalized buttons for controlling various software applications and functions, making it ideal for streamers, content creators, and power users.

To launch an Automation Blocks tool, use the [System Action - Website](https://help.elgato.com/hc/en-us/articles/360028234471-Elgato-Stream-Deck-System-Actions-Hotkey-Open-Website-Multimedia#h_01G93JMZXBEAT71H4NSYM2YTXE)
 and make sure to check the option "GET Request in background", such that the URL is not opened in your default webbrowser, but only opened in the background.

### Touch Portal[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#touchportal "Direct link to heading")

[Touch Portal](https://www.touch-portal.com/)
 is a mobile app that transforms your smartphone or tablet into a customizable control surface, enabling users to remotely trigger actions, macros, and commands on their computer, making it versatile for streamers, video editors, and automation enthusiasts.

To execute an Automation Blocks script with Touch Portal, just configure a button with a "Http Get" action and enter the remote URL for the script you want to execute:

![Execute Automation Blocks script with Touch Portal in Premiere Pro and After Effects](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/remoteExecution/example_touch_portal.png)

Remote Execution via CEP Events[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#remote-execution-via-cep-events "Direct link to heading")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You can also remote-execute Automation Blocks scripts from other extensions (CEP panels). If you develop your own extensions, there is no need to reinvent the wheel: Just use Automation Blocks inside your own extensions and speed up your development.

##### new in Automation Blocks 1.0.012

CEP events have been added in Autommation Blocks 1.0.012. If you use an older version, make sure to update!

### Run Script File[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#run-script-file "Direct link to heading")

_Event for Pr: `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.runScriptFile`  
Event for Ae: `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksAe.runScriptFile`_

To execute an Automation Blocks script, just send a CSEvent with type `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.runScriptFile` in Premiere Pro or `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksAe.runScriptFile` in After Effects.

In the event data, you need to provide a `filePath`, to specify the xml file containing the block script you want to execute:

    // execute script test.xml without loading it into the UIvar event = new CSEvent('com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.runScriptFile', 'APPLICATION');event.data = {    filePath: "/path/to/file/test.xml"};new CSInterface().dispatchEvent(event);

In order to make this work, Automation Blocks needs to be installed (and licensed) on the machine, but the Automation Blocks user interface does not need to be open.

If you run into any issues open http://localhost:2029/ with Chrome to see the debugger of the Automation Blocks API. Automation Blocks prints status messages in this console when it receives the event and also shows the errors there, if something went wrong.

#### Callback[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callback "Direct link to heading")

If you [register a callback](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callbacks)
, then Automation Blocks will send an event back to you after it executed the script (or if that failed). The data sent with the callback event will contain the following properties:

*   **status** will be `ERROR` if loading or executing the script failed and `SUCCESS` otherwise.
*   **result** will be an error message if status is `ERROR`. Otherwise it will contain the result of the script execution.

##### Input Blocks Limitation

Since this event executes the block scripts without a user interface, [input blocks](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/userInterfaceAndComments#input_arg)
 will always use their default value. If you need user input, use the other user interface blocks, which create popup dialogs.

### Run Current Script[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#run-current-script "Direct link to heading")

_Event for Pr: `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.runCurrentScript`  
Event for Ae: `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksAe.runCurrentScript`_

If the user interface of Automation Blocks is open , you can also ask it to run the block script, which is currently open in the UI:

    // run the script that is currently open in the Automation Blocks UIvar event = new CSEvent('com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.runCurrentScript', 'APPLICATION');new CSInterface().dispatchEvent(event);

This event is handled by the panel showing the Automation Blocks UI. Hence, it only works if the Automation Blocks UI is open. For debugging open the Automation Blocks panel at http://localhost:2026/ (note the 2026 instead of 2029!).

#### Callback[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callback-1 "Direct link to heading")

If you [register a callback](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callbacks)
, the data sent with the callback event will contain the following properties:

*   **status** will be `ERROR` if the script execution failed and `SUCCESS` otherwise.
*   **result** will be an error message if status is `ERROR`. Otherwise it will contain the result of the script execution.

### Load Script File[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#load-script-file "Direct link to heading")

_Event for Pr: `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.loadScriptFile`  
Event for Ae: `com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksAe.loadScriptFile`_

If the user interface of Automation Blocks is open, you can also tell it to open a block script of your choice in the user interface:

    // load the block script test.xml into the Automation. Blocks UIvar event = new CSEvent('com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.loadScriptFile', 'APPLICATION');event.data = {    filePath: "/path/to/file/test.xml"};new CSInterface().dispatchEvent(event);

This event is handled by the panel showing the Automation Blocks UI. Hence, it only works if the Automation Blocks UI is open. For debugging open the Automation Blocks panel at http://localhost:2026/.

#### Callback[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callback-2 "Direct link to heading")

If you [register a callback](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callbacks)
, the data sent with the callback event will contain the following properties:

*   **status** will be `ERROR` if loading the script failed and `SUCCESS` otherwise.
*   **result** will be an error message if status is `ERROR`. Otherwise it will be the empty string.

### Callbacks[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#callbacks "Direct link to heading")

When you send an event to Automation Blocks, you can also request Automation Blocks to send an answer back to your CEP panel. For this, just add an object `callbackData` to the event data with the following properties:

*   _eventType_: A string for the type of event you want to recieve. Make sure to choose a unique name, which does not overlap with events from other extensions.
*   _customData_: Here you can pass any json object. Automation Blocks will send this data back with the reply. You can use this to pass unique IDs for each event, for example, such that you know to which your your events the reply belongs.

Here is an example:

    // register an event handler to recieve the reply from Automation Blocksnew CSInterface().addEventListener(  "my_reply_callback_unique_name",  function (event) {    var data = event.data;    console.log("recieved reply event:", event.data);  });// now send your eventvar myEvent = new CSEvent(    "com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksPr.runScriptFile",    "APPLICATION");myEvent.data = {    filePath: "path/to/test.xml",    callbackData: {      eventType: "my_reply_callback_unique_name",      customData: {        id: 1234,        whatever: "foo",      },    },};new CSInterface().dispatchEvent(myEvent);

ExtendScript, Kbar & Quick Menu[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#kbarquickmemu "Direct link to heading")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You can also send the events from After Effects scripts (ExtendScript). This is in particular useful, since launcher tools like [KBar](https://aescripts.com/kbar/)
 or [Quick Menu](https://aescripts.com/quick-menu/)
 can execute custom scripts.

The syntax for ExtendScript is a little bit different, since you need to load the PlugPlugExternalObject library and also need to _JSON.stringify_ your data. You can use the script code below to launch an Automation Blocks script from Kbar's "Run Scriptlet", for example. You can also save the code to a jsx file and run it from the Ae menu "File->Scripts->Run Script File" or from QuickMenu.

    var xLib;try {    xLib = new ExternalObject("lib:\PlugPlugExternalObject");} catch(e) { alert("Missing ExternalObject: "+e); }if (xLib) {    var eventObj = new CSXSEvent();    eventObj.type = 'com.aescripts.AutomationBlocksAe.runScriptFile';    eventObj.data = JSON.stringify({        filePath: "/path/to/file/test.xml"    });    eventObj.dispatch();}

Just replace `"/path/to/file/test.xml"` by the file path of the block script you want to execute.

*   [Remote Execution via HTTP Requests](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#remote-execution-via-http-requests)
    *   [Enabling HTTP Request Execution](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#enabling-http-request-execution)
        
    *   [How It Works](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/remoteExecution/#howitworks)

# Files Spreadsheet | Automation Blocks

[Skip to main content](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#)

On this page

Files Spreadsheet
=================

These blocks allow you to read and write spreadsheet files. We currently support two formats:

*   csv (supported by all major spreadsheet applications like Microsoft Excel, Open Office, Apple Numbers and Google Docs)
*   Excel XML

Whenever you read or write a spreadsheet file, make sure it has either the file extension .csv or .xml. Based on this file extension, Automation Blocks will decide whether it treats the file as a csv or an Excel XML file.

Add Row/Column to Spreadsheet[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_add_row "Direct link to heading")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block spreadsheet_add_row](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/FilesSpreadsheet/spreadsheet_add_row.png)

Adds a new row (or column) to a spreadsheet file.

By default, the values for the first two cells (cell A and cell B) in the new row are written. You can add more cells by clicking on the blue cog wheel icon in the top left corner of the block.

##### Creating New Files

If the spreadsheet file already exists, this block will append a new row/column to it. If you want to start a new file, just [delete the existing file](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/files#block_extendscript_delete_file)
 before this block.

For Each Row/Column of Spreadsheet[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_for_each_row "Direct link to heading")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block spreadsheet_for_each_row](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/FilesSpreadsheet/spreadsheet_for_each_row.png)

Reads an entire spreadsheet file row by row (or column by column).

With the optional `from row` and `to row` parameters you can limit the range of rows that is read. If the first row of your spreadsheet contains a title that you want to skip, for example, just set `from row` to `2` (cell indices start at 1 for the first row).

By default, from each row the first two cells are read (cell A and cell B), but if you click on the cogwheel icon in the top left corner of the block, you can add more cells.

The blocks that you nest inside this block are executed once for each row of the spreadsheet. During that execution, the contents of each cell in that row is stored in a [variable](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/variables)
 (`my cell 1`, `my cell 2` etc.) so that you can easily access the contents of these cells.

Set Spreadsheet Cell[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_set_cell "Direct link to heading")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block spreadsheet_set_cell](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/FilesSpreadsheet/spreadsheet_set_cell.png)

Writes a single cell to a spreadsheet. If the file already exists, the content of this cell will be replaced.

Cell indices start at 1 for the first row/column.

Get Spreadsheet Cell[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_get_cell "Direct link to heading")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block spreadsheet_get_cell](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/FilesSpreadsheet/spreadsheet_get_cell.png)

Reads a single cell from a spreadsheet. If the cell is empty, the empty text will be returned.

Cell indices start at 1 for the first row/column.

Get Size of Spreadsheet[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_get_cell "Direct link to heading")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![Block spreadsheet_get_size](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/img/blockScreenshots/FilesSpreadsheet/spreadsheet_get_size.png)

Returns the number of rows or the number of columns of the spreadsheet file.

Troubleshooting Spreadsheet Issues[​](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#troubleshooting-spreadsheet-issues "Direct link to heading")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Here you find some help from the community, if you run into issues with reading or writing spreadsheets:

*   [Troubleshooting opening CSV files in your spreadsheet application](https://community.adobe.com/t5/premiere-pro-discussions/automation-blocks-spreadsheet-being-generated-with-combined-columns/m-p/14538670)
    
*   [Troubleshooting importing CSV Spreadsheets](https://community.adobe.com/t5/premiere-pro-discussions/automation-blocks-import-properties-of-clips-from-spreadsheet/m-p/14489330)
    

*   [Add Row/Column to Spreadsheet](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_add_row)
    
*   [For Each Row/Column of Spreadsheet](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_for_each_row)
    
*   [Set Spreadsheet Cell](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_set_cell)
    
*   [Get Spreadsheet Cell](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_get_cell)
    
*   [Get Size of Spreadsheet](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#spreadsheet_get_cell)
    
*   [Troubleshooting Spreadsheet Issues](https://docs.mamoworld.com/automation-blocks/block-reference/filesSpreadsheet/#troubleshooting-spreadsheet-issues)
